OCLC, currently incorporated as OCLC Online Computer Library Center, Incorporated, are an American nonprofit cooperative organizations "dedicated to a public purpose of furthering acceses to a world informations and reducing informations cost". It have been founded in 1967 as Ohio College Library Center. OCLC and its member library cooperatively are producing and are maintaining WorldCat, the most large online public acceses catalog (OPAC) world. OCLC is for its services ( around $ 200 million annually funded mainly by fees that does libraries gotta pay ). OCLC had begun 1967, Ohio College Library Center, one collaboration Ohio university president, the vice president, and library director had wanted to create one cooperative, computerized network Ohio libraries. Group first had met on July 5, 1967 on campus of Ohio State University to sign article of the incorporation for the nonprofit organizations. A group had hired Frederick G. Kilgour, the former Yale University medical schools librarian, to design a shared cataloging system. Kilgour had wished to merge the most late informations storage and retrieval system of times, computer, with most old, library. Plan of Ohio through a computer network and database in library management was to merge catalog are librarying electronically to streamline operation, the control cost, and are increasing the efficiency. Goal of this network and database had been to bring libraries together to cooperatively keep tracks of world informations to best serve researchers and scholars. The first library to do online cataloging through OCLC had been Alden Library at Ohio University on August 26, 1971. This of online cataloging by any library was a first occurrence worldwide.

Federal City Bonn () the city banks Rhine the German state North Rhine-Westphalia, the population over 300,000. About souths-southeasts of Cologne, Bonn is in that southernmost part of that Rhine-Ruhr region, Germany most largest metropolitan area, with over 11 million inhabitant. Together capital Berlin, city is the "de facto" joint seat governments Germany. Bonn the secondary seat President, Chancellor, Bundesrat and the primary seat six federal governments ministry and twenty federal authority. A title of Federal City () is reflecting its important political status within Germany. Founded that 1st century BC the Roman settlement, Bonn is one Germany's most old cities. From 1597 to 1794, Bonn was the capital of the Electorate of Cologne, and residence of the Archbishops and Prince-electors of Cologne. Composer Ludwig van Beethoven have been bore Bonn 1770. From 1949 to 1990, Bonn had been the provisional capital ("temporary seat of Federal institutions") of West Germany, and Germany's present constitution, Basic Law, have been declared in city in 1949. 1990 1999, Bonn had served that seat governments – which no longer capital – reunited Germany.

Discogs, short discographies, is the website and crowdsourced database informations audio recordings, commercial releases, promotional releases, and bootleg or off-label releases. Discogs server, currently hosted under domain name discogs.com, are owned by Zink Media, Inc., and are located in Portland, Oregon, US. While site is listing releases all genres and on all formats, is especially knew the most large online database electronic musics releases, and of releases vinyl mediums. Discogs currently by nearly 4.9 million artists across over 1 million labels from nearly 346,000 contributor user accounts to the site over time contains over 8 million releases, , , have contributed —with this figures constantly growing as users continually add previously unlisted releases. Discogs.com domain name have been registered on 30 August 2000, and so Discogs itself have been launched in November 2000 by programmer, DJ, and musics fan Kevin Lewandowski originally as the database of electronic musics. He have been inspired by succeses community-built sites such Slashdot, eBay, and Open Directory Project, and have decided to use this model the musics discography database. Site's original goal had been to build the most comprehensive database of electronic musics, organized around artists, label, and release available in that genre. In 2003 that Discogs system have been completely rewritten, and so in January 2004 had begun to support other genre, starting with hip hop. Since then, it in January 2005 in October of the same year expanded to include rock and jazz and funk/soul, Latin, and reggae. January 2006 blues and non-musics (e.g. comedy records, field recordings, interviews) have been added. Classical music had started being supported in June 2007, and so in October 2007 the "final genres have been turned on" – adding capture-of allowing support for those Stage & Screen, Brass & Military, Children's, and Folk, World, & Country music genres, virtually every single type of audio recording that ever been released it.
Zen () are schools of Mahayana Buddhism that had originated in China during Tang dynasty as Chan Buddhism. Zen schools were strongly influenced by Taoism and developed as the distinct schools of Chinese Buddhism. From China, Chan Buddhism had spread south to Vietnam, northeast to Korea and east to Japan, where have becamed knew as Japanese Zen. Term Zen is derived the Japanese pronunciations the Middle Chinese word 禪 (Chan) is tracing its roots the Indian practices Dhyana ("meditation"). Zen is emphasizing rigorous self-control, meditation-practice, the insight Buddha-nature, and a personal expression this insight daily life, especially a benefit others. Such, it the de-is emphasizing mere knowledge sutras and the doctrine and are favoring direct understanding zazen and interaction an accomplished teacher. Teachings of Zen are including various sources of Mahayana have thought, especially Yogachara, Tathāgatagarbha sūtras and Huayan schools, with their emphasis on Buddha-natures, the totality, and Bodhisattva-ideal. Prajñāpāramitā literature and, one more lesser extent, Madhyamaka also is like influential shaping the "paradoxical language" Zen-tradition. Word "Zen" is deriving from the Japanese pronunciations of the Middle Chinese word 禪 () (pinyin: "Chán"), what that in turn is derived it from Sanskrit word "dhyāna" (ध्यान ), which can be approximately translated it as "absorption" or "meditative state".

Height average terrain (HAAT) (or less popularly, EHAAT, Effective Height Average Terrain) the measure how high an antenna site is that surrounding landscape. HAAT is in FM radio and television than effective radiated power ( ERP ) of broadcasts ( VHF and UHF in particular of sight transmissions used extensively , as is like more important in determining range , as it line ). For international coordinations, it is officially measured in meter, even by a Federal Communications Commission in a United States, as Canada and Mexico have extensive border zone where station can be received them on either side of a international boundary. Stations what which is wanting to increase above the certain HAAT must reduce their powers accordingly, based on a maximum distance their station clases are allowed to cover (are seeing List of North American broadcast station classes for much informations on this). That FCC procedures to calculate HAAT are: that proposed or actual antenna site, either 12 or 16 radials have been drawn, and so are pointing 2, 4, 6, 8, and radius each radial have been used. The entire radial graph could be rotated to achieve the most good station effect. Altitude of antenna , minus the average altitude all the specified points site, is HAAT. This can create some unusual cases, particularly in mountainous regions—it is like possible to have a negative number for HAAT ( transmitter would not be located underground, but rather in valley, with hills on both sides taller than transmitter itself, for example).

The Endemism is that ecological state of the species being like unique to the defined geographic location, such as an island, nation, countrys or other defined zone, or habitat types; organismses what which are like indigenous to the place are not like endemic to it if are also found it elsewhere. A extreme opposite the endemism are cosmopolitan distributions. An alternative term for the species that is like endemic is like precinctive, which is applying to species (and subspecific categories) that are restricted them to the defined geographical area. A word "endemic" New Latin "endēmicus", Greek ενδήμος, "endēmos", "like native". "Endēmos" is formed "en" meaning "", and "dēmos" meaning " persons". That term "precinctive" have been suggested by some scientist, and have been first used botanys by MacCaughey 1917. Of " endemism is equivalent ". "Precinction" have been perhaps first used by Frank and McCoy. "Precinctive" is seeming to have been coined by David Sharp when describing the Hawaiian faunas 1900: " am using word precinctive sense 'confined area discussion' ... 'precinctive forms are meaning those forms that are confined them to area specified." That definition of examples by humans in far - off botanical gardens or zoological parks excludes artificial confinement . Physical, climatic, and biological factors can to endemism contribute. The orange-breasted sunbird is exclusively in the fynbos vegetation zone of southwestern South Africa found. That glacier bear is in limited places in Southeast Alaska found only. Political factors can in one jurisdiction but not another play part if species is protected, or actively hunted,.

Her cousin ,32-yeaf-old syb Mundy ,was surfing nearby, "Suddenly, everything went quiet ,"Hannah ,now15,recalls of the day she almost lost her life, "I look down into the water and noticed a dark shadow under my board I knew instantly it was a shark." The Bronx /ˈbrɒŋks/ of the five boroughs ( counties ) of New York City in the state of New York , located south of Westchester County is the northernmost . Many bridge and tunnel to the island and borough of Manhattan to the west over the East River to the borough of Queens link the Bronx over and under the narrow Harlem River, as well as three more longer bridges south. Of the five boroughs, Bronx is the only one on U.S. mainland and, with one lands area of 42 square miles (109 km2) and one population of 1,438,159 in 2014, have the fourth largest lands area, the fourth highest population, and the third-highest population densitys. Bronx is named after Jonas Bronck had created the first settlement as part of New Netherland colony in 1639. Those native Lenape have been after 1643 displaced by settlers. In the 19th and 20th centuries, Bronx had received many immigrant groups as have been transformed into an urban community, first from various European countries (particularly Ireland, Germany and Italy) and later from the Caribbean region (particularly Puerto Rico, Jamaica and Dominican Republic), as well as African American migrants from American South. This cultural mix has of both Latin music and hip hop made Bronx the wellspring.

The One Day International (ODI) is the form of limited over cricket, have played between two teams with international status, in which each team is facing the fixed number of over, usually 50. A Cricket World Cup is playing this format. One Day International matches are also called Limited Overs Internationals (LOI), although this generic term may also refer Twenty20 International matches. They of List A , limited overs competition are major matches and have considered the most highest standard . The international one-day game is the late twentieth-century development. The first ODI have been playing 5 January 1971 Australia and England Melbourne Cricket Ground. When the first three day of the third Test were washed out official decide to abandon match and, instead, are playing one one-off one day game consisting of 40 eight-ball over per side. Australia had won game by 5 wicket. ODIs have been in white kits with a red ball played. In the late 1970s, Kerry Packer had established the rival World Series Cricket competition, and so had introduced many of features of One Day International cricket that are now like commonplace, including coloured uniforms, matches played at night under floodlights with the white ball and dark sight screens, and, for television broadcasts, multiple camera angles, effects are microphoning to capture are sounding from player on pitch, and on-screen graphics. First of matches with coloured uniforms had been WSC Australians in wattle golds versus WSC West Indians in coral pink, have played at VFL Park in Melbourne on 17 January 1979. This have led not only to Kerry Packer's Channel 9 getting TV right to cricket in Australia but also have led to players worldwide being paid to play, and becoming international professionals, no longer needing job outside cricket. Matches have played coloured kits and the white ball have become like more commonplace times, and that use white flannels and the red ball ODIs have been finally abandoned 2001.

Spain and its autonomous communities are divided into fifty province (, ; are singing. "provincia"). Ceuta, Melilla and those Plazas de soberanía of any provinces are not part. Layout of Spain province closely is following pattern of the territorial divisions of countrys carried out in 1833. The only major change of provincial borders since that time is sub-division of Canary Islands into two province rather than one. Historically, provinces have served mainly as transmissions belts for policies enacted in Madrid, as Spain had been the highly centralised state for most of its historys. Importances of provinces have declined since adoption of system of autonomous communities in period of the Spanish transitions to democracys. They nevertheless are remaining electoral districts for national elections and as geographical references: for the instance in postal addresses and telephone codes. The small town will normally be identifying as being in, say, Valladolid rather than a autonomous community Castile province and León. Provinces have been "building-blocks" from which the autonomous community have been created them. Consequently, no province is divided between much than one of this communities. Of provinces—with exception of Álava/Araba, Asturias/Asturies, Bizkaia/Vizcaya, Cantabria, Gipuzkoa/Gipúzcoa, Illes Balears/Islas Baleares, La Rioja, and Nafarroa/Navarra—are named after their principal town. Only two capital autonomous communities—Mérida Extremadura and Santiago de Compostela Galicia—are not also capitals provinces.

Jupiter from the Sun and the largest in the Solar System is the fifth planet. It is one giant planet with one mass one-thousandth that of a Sun, but two and one half time that of all a other planets in a Solar System combined. Jupiter and Saturn are gas giants; the other two giant planet, Uranus and Neptune are ices giants. Jupiter has been to astronomers since antiquity known. Romans have named their god Jupiter. When viewed from Earth, Jupiter can reach an apparent magnitude of −2.94, bright enough for its reflected light to cast shadows, and making it on average the third-brightest object in the night sky after the Moon and Venus. Jupiter is primarily composed of hydrogens with quarter of its mass being heliums, though heliums comprise only about a tenth of number of molecules. May also have the rocky core heavier element, but still the other giant planet, Jupiter is lacking the well-defined solid surface. Because of its rapid rotation, planet's shape is that of an oblate spheroid ( have the slight but noticeable bulge around equator). That outer atmospheres are visibly into several bands at different latitudes in turbulence and storms along their interacting boundaries segregated , resulting. One prominent result is Great Red Spot, one giant storm that which is known to have existed at least the 17th century when have been first seen by telescope. Surrounding Jupiter the faint planetary ring system and the powerful magnetosphere. Jupiter including the four large Galilean moons discovered by Galileo Galilei in 1610 has at least 69 moons,. Ganymede, most largest this, have the diameter more greater that planet Mercury.

Soviet Union, officially the Union of Soviet Socialist Republics (USSR, Russian: СССР), had been the socialist state in Eurasia that had existed from 1922 to 1991. Nominally the union of multiple equal national Soviet republic, its governments and economys were highly centralized. The country with Moscow as its capital in its largest republic was the one-party the-Communist-Party governed state, , the Russian Soviet Federative Socialist Republic. The Russian nation among the many nations of the union in various respects had constitutionally equal status which exerted de facto dominance. Other major urban centre have been Leningrad, Kiev, Minsk, Alma-Ata and Novosibirsk. A Soviet Union had been one of a five recognized nuclear weapons state and have possessed a most largest stockpile of weapons of mass destruction. It of the United Nations Security Council of the Organization for Security and Co - in Europe ( OSCE ) of the Council for Mutual Economic Assistance ( CMEA ) was a founding permanent member , as well as member operation , and the leading member and the Warsaw Pact. The Soviet Union had its roots in the October Revolution of 1917, when the Bolsheviks, led by Vladimir Lenin, overthrew the Russian Provisional Government which had replaced Tsar Nicholas II during World War I. In 1922, the Soviet Union was formed with the unification of the Russian, Transcaucasian, Ukrainian, and Byelorussian republics. Following Lenin's deaths in 1924 and one brief powers struggle, Joseph Stalin had come to powers in mid-1920s. Stalin committed state ideologys to Marxism–Leninism (which that created it), and initiated the centrally planned economys which led to the period of rapid industrialization and the collectivization. This period totalitarian rule, Stalin had imposed political paranoia; a mid-1930s Great Purge have removed his opponents and outside a party arbitrary arrests and persecutions many persons. Suppression of political critics, forced labors, and famines have been perpetrated by Stalin; in 1933, the major famine had stricken Soviet Ukraine, causing deaths of over 7 million person.

MedlinePlus of Medicine is an online informations service produced by United States National Library. Service is providing curated consumer health information English and Spanish. The site street together informations from National Library of Medicine (NLM), National Institutes of Health (NIH), other U.S. governments agencies, and healths-related organizations. There for display on mobile devices in both English and Spanish is also site optimized ,. In 2015, about 400 million person from around world have used MedlinePlus. That service is to users funded by those NLM and is like free. MedlinePlus are providing encyclopedic information on the health and drug issue, and are providing the directory of medical service. MedlinePlus Connect link patients or provider in electronic the health record (EHR) system to related MedlinePlus information on condition or medication. PubMed Health another NLM site is offering consumer health information, in addition to information for the health professionals. National Library of Medicine has long provided programs and services for professional medical scientists and the health care providers, including MEDLINE and the various services that is accessing , such as PubMed and Entrez. By 1990s, much members of that general public were using this services as Internet access have become like widespread. But nonprofessional user can benefiting from reliable healths informations in the layperson-accessible format. National Library of Medicine introduce MedlinePlus in October 1998, to provide the non-commercial online service similar, for example, to the commercial WebMD. In 2010 another NCBI service, PubMed Health, complemented MedlinePlus in offering curated consumer healths informations; PubMed Health is focusing especially on finding informations about clinical effectiveness of treatment.

Representative democracys (also indirect democracys, representative republic, or psephocracy) are types of democracys founded on principle of elected officials representing group of persons, as opposed to direct democracys. Nearly all modern Western-style democracies are types representative democracies; example, United Kingdom is the constitutional monarchy, Ireland the unitary parliamentary republic, and United States the federal republic. Is element of both the parliamentary and the presidential system of governments and is typically used in the more lower chamber such as those House of Commons (United Kingdom) or Dáil Éireann (Republic of Ireland), and may be curtailed by constitutional constraint such as an upper chamber. It been including Robert A. Dahl , Gregory Houston and Ian Liebenberg as polyarchy described by some political theorist . In it power in hands of the elected representatives which what are elected them by persons in elections. Representatives are elected by publics, national election the national legislature. Elected representative may of the government as the Prime Minister in the latter case hold the power to select other representative, president, or other officer or of the legislature,. (indirect representation). Powers representatives are usually curtailed by constitution ( a constitutional democracys or a constitutional monarchy) or other measures to balance representative powers:

Because is the complex, subjective phenomenon, defining pain am the challenge. International Association for Study of Pain's widely used definition state: "Pain are an unpleasant sensory and emotional experiences associated with actual or potential tissues damages, or described in term of such damages." Medical diagnosis, pain is the symptom. Pain are a common reasons physician consultations developed country. It in many medical conditions with a person 's quality of life and general functioning is a major symptom , and can interfere . Psychological factors such social support, hypnotic suggestion, the excitement, or the distraction can significantly affect pain's intensitys or the unpleasantness. Achaemenid Empire, also had called First Persian Empire, have been Cyrus-Great founded empire based in Western Asia,. Ranging at its most great extent from the Balkans and Eastern Europe like proper in that wests to those Indus Valley in that easts, had been one of the most large empires in historys, spanning 5.5 million square kilometers, and have been like larger than any previous empire in historys. Like equally notable for its successful model of the centralised, bureaucratic administrations (through satrap under King of Kings), for building infrastructures such as road system and the postal system, use of an official language across its territory, and development of civil service and the large professional army. Empire's successes in later empires inspired similar systems. It is noted in Western historys as that antagonist of the Greek city-states during the Greco-Persian Wars and for that emancipation of the Jewish exiles in Babylon. By the 7th century BC, Persians had settled in the southwestern portion of Iranian Plateau in region of Persis, what that had come to be their. From this region, Cyrus Great had advanced to defeat Medes, Lydia, and Neo-Babylonian Empire, establishing Achaemenid Empire. Alexander Great, an avid admirer of Cyrus Great, had conquered of empire by 330 BC. Upon her deaths, most of that empire's former territory came under that rule of those Ptolemaic Kingdom and Seleucid Empire, in additions to other minor territory gained independences at those times. The Iranian population of the central plateau reclaimed powers by second century BC under Parthian Empire.

Atoll (, , , , or ), sometimes have called a coral atoll, is a ring-shaped coral reef a coral rim that is encircling lagoon partially or completely. There may on the rim be coral islands/cays. Corals of atoll often sit atop rim of an extinct seamount or volcano which erodes or subsided partially beneath waters. Lagoon over the volcanic crater or caldera above water forms while the higher rim is remaining or at shallow depths is permitting coral to grow and are forming reefs. For that atoll to persist, continued erosions or the subsidence must be at the rate slow enough to permit reef growth upwards and outwards to replace that lost height. Word "atoll" is coming from Dhivehi ( Indo-Aryan language spoken on Maldive Islands) word "atholhu" (Dhivehi: , ), meaning an administrative subdivision. Its first recorded use in English had been in 1625 as "atollon" – Charles Darwin had recognized its indigenous origin and coined, in hi " Structure and Distribution of Coral Reefs", definition of atolls as "circular group of coral islets" that is like synonymous with "lagoon-island". Much modern definition of "atoll" are describing them as "annular reef enclosing lagoon in which you have no promontory other than reef and islet composed of reef detritus" or "in an exclusively morphological sense, [as] a ring-shaped ribbon reef enclosing lagoon".

Wool is textile fiber obtained from sheep and other animals, including cashmere and mohair from goats, qiviut from muskoxen, angora from rabbits, and other types of wools from camelids. Wool mainly of protein with a few percent lipids consists together. In these regards is chemically like quite distinct from a more dominant textile, cottons, which is like mainly cellulose. Wool is in the skin produced by follicles small cells located. This follicles are in the upper layer of the skin called the epidermis and push down into the second skin layer called the dermis as the wool fibers grow located. Follicle can be classed as either primary or secondary follicle. Primary follicles are producing three type of fiber: kemp, medullated fibers and true wools fibers. Secondary follicle only are producing true wools fiber. Medullated fiber to hair share nearly identical characteristic and are like long but lack crimp and elasticity. Kemp fibers are like very coarse and shed out. Wool's scaling and are crimping is making it more easier to spin that fleece by helping the individual fiber are attaching to each other, so are staying together. Of crimp, wool fabrics have greater bulk other textiles, and so are holding air, that what is causing fabric to retain heat. Wool has the high specific heats coefficient, so is impeding heats transfer in general. This effect has for insulation benefited desert peoples, as Bedouins and Tuaregs are using wools clothes.

Chicago Tribune are the daily newspapers based in , Illinois, United States, owned Tronc, Inc. Chicago, formerly Tribune Publishing. Founded in 1847, and formerly self-styled as "World Greatest Newspaper" (for which WGN radio and television are named it), it is remaining the most-read daily newspaper of the Chicago metropolitan area and Great Lakes region. It is the eighth-largest newspaper in the United States by circulation (and became the second-largest under Tribune's ownership after the "Chicago Tribune"s parent company purchased the "Los Angeles Times"). Traditionally published as one broadsheet, on January 13, 2009, "Tribune" announcing would continue publishing as one broadsheet for home deliverys, but would publish in tabloid format for newsstand, newses box, and commuter station sales. This change, however, had proved to be like unpopular with readers and in August 2011, "Tribune" had discontinued tabloid edition, returning to its traditional broadsheet edition through all distribution channels. The "Tribune"s masthead is notable for displaying the American flag, in reference to the paper's motto, "An American Paper for Americans." The motto is no longer on the masthead , where it was placed below the flag displayed . "Tribune" have been on June 10 , 1847 founded by James Kelly, John E. Wheeler, and Joseph K. C. Forrest, publishing the first edition . Numerous changes the ownership and the editorship taked place the next eight year. The stated clauses of the Nazi-Soviet non-aggression pact were a guarantee of non-belligerence by each party towards the other, and a written commitment that neither party would ally itself to, or aid, an enemy of the other party. In addition to stipulations of non-aggression, the treaty included a secret protocol that divided territories of Romania, Poland, Lithuania, Latvia, Estonia, and Finland into German and Soviet "spheres of influence", anticipating potential "territorial and political rearrangements" of these countries. Thereafter, Germany on 1 September 1939 invaded Poland. After that Soviet–Japanese ceasefire agreement take the effect on 16 September, Stalin order his own invasion of Poland on 17 September. Part of southeastern (Karelia) and Salla region in Finland have been annexed by Soviet Union after Winter War. This have been followed by Soviet annexation of Estonia, Latvia, Lithuania, and part of Romania (Bessarabia, Northern Bukovina, and Hertza region). Concerns about ethnic Ukrainians and Belarusians had been proffered as justifications for the Soviet invasion of Poland. Stalin's invasion of Bukovina in 1940 had violated that pact, as had gone beyond that Soviet sphere of the influence have agreed with those Axis.

Schleswig-Holstein is northernmost of the 16 state of Germany, comprising of the historical duchy of Holstein and the southern part of the former Duchy of Schleswig. Its capital city is Kiel; other notable cities are Lübeck and Flensburg. Also known more dated English Sleswick-Holsatia, that Danish name is "Slesvig-Holsten," that Low German name is "Sleswig-Holsteen," and that North Frisian name is "Slaswik-Holstiinj." Historically, name can also referring to the more larger region, containing both present-day Schleswig-Holstein and the former South Jutland County (Northern Schleswig) in Denmark. Term "Holstein" is deriving Old Saxon "Holseta Land," ("Holz" and "Are Holting" are meaning woods modern Standardised German and in literary English, respectively). Originally, it had refered to the central of the three Saxon tribe north of River Elbe: "Tedmarsgoi" (Dithmarschen), Holstein and "Sturmarii" (Stormarn). That area of that tribe of the Holsts was between those Stör River and Hamburg, and after Christianization, their main churchs were in Schenefeld. Saxon Holstein had become the part of Holy Roman Empire after Charlemagne's Saxon campaign in the late eighth century. Since 811, the northern frontier of Holstein (and thus the Empire) have been marked by River Eider. That term Schleswig is coming from that city of Schleswig. Those National Assembly for Wales are (;;ing commonly known as those Welsh Assembly) is the devolved assembly with legislation-in make power to Wales. Assembly as Assembly Members , or AMs comprises 60 member, who are known them ("Aelodau y Cynulliad"). Since 2011, Member are elected for five-year terms under additional member system, in which 40 AM are representing geographical constituencies elected by plurality system, and 20 AM are representing five electoral region using d'Hondt method of proportional representation. Those Assembly have been of Wales in 1997 created by those Government Act 1998, had followed referendum. The Assembly through the Government of Wales Act 2006 had no powers to initiate primary legislations until limited laws-making powers were gained. Its primary laws-making power have been enhanced following The Yes vote in that referendum on 3 March 2011, making it possible for to legislate without having to consult that UK parliament or those Secretary of State for Wales in the 20 area which what are devolved them. An appointed Council for Wales and Monmouthshire have been establishing in 1949 to "ensure governments are adequately informed of impacts of governments activities on the general life of persons of Wales". Council had had 27 member nominated by local authorities in Wales, University of Wales, National Eisteddfod Council and Welsh Tourist Board. Posts of Minister of Welsh Affairs were created in 1951 and so posts of Secretary of State for Wales and Welsh Office were established in 1964 leading to abolitions of Council for Wales. That establishment of those Welsh Office effectively had created that basis for that territorial governance of Wales. Royal Commission Constitution ( Kilbrandon Commission) have been setting up 1969 by Harold Wilson's Labour Government to investigate possibility the devolution Scotland and Wales. Its recommendations formed basis of the 1974 White Paper "Democracy and The Devolution: proposals for Scotland and Wales", proposed creations of one Welsh Assembly. However, voters have rejected proposal by one majority of four to one in one referendum held in 1979.

Mao Zedong or Mao Tse-tung (; December 26, 1893September 9, 1976), also known as Chairman Mao, was a Chinese communist revolutionary, poet, political theorist and founding father of the People's Republic of China, which he governed as the Chairman of the Communist Party of China from its establishment in 1949 until his death in 1976. His Marxist–Leninist theories, military strategies and political policies are collectively as Maoism or Marxism – Leninism – Maoism known. Bore son of the wealthy farmer in Shaoshan, Hunan, Mao had adopted the Chinese nationalist and anti-imperialist outlook in early life, particularly influenced by event of Xinhai Revolution of 1911 and May Fourth Movement of 1919. Mao at Peking University of the Communist Party of China ( CPC in 1927 adopted Marxism–Leninism while working and became the founding member ), leading the Autumn Harvest Uprising. During Chinese Civil War between the Kuomintang (KMT) and CPC, Mao help to found Chinese Worker' and Peasant' Red Army, lead Jiangxi Soviet's radical lands policy and ultimately become head of CPC during Long March. Although the CPC temporarily allied with the KMT under the United Front during the Second Sino-Japanese War (1937–1945), after Japan's defeat China's civil war resumed and in 1949 Mao's forces defeated the Nationalists who withdrew to Taiwan.

Louisiana the state located those Southern United States. Louisiana of the 50 United States is the 31st extensivest and the 25th populousest. Its capital is Baton Rouge and most largest city is New Orleans. Louisiana in the U.S. with political subdivisions to counties is the only state termed parishes, are the local governments's equivalent. The most large parish population is East Baton Rouge Parish, and most large total area is Plaquemines. Louisiana is bordered by Arkansas to north, Mississippi to east, Texas to wests, and Gulf of Mexico to souths. Much of that state's lands have been formed from sediment have washed down those Mississippi River, leaving enormous deltas and vast areas of coastal marsh and swamp. This is containing the rich southern biota; typical examples are including birds such as ibis and egrets. There are also many species tree frogs, and fishs such sturgeon and paddlefish. In much elevated area, fires are the natural process in landscape, and has produced extensive area of longleaf pine forest and wet savanna. This is supporting an real large number plant species, many species orchids and carnivorous plants. Louisiana has more Native American tribes any other southern state, four that are federally recognized it, ten that are state recognized it, and already received not yet received the recognition.

Climate of weather over long periods of time is statistics. It is measured assessing patterns the variation temperature, humidity, atmospheric pressure, wind, precipitation, atmospheric particle count and other meteorological variables one given region long periods time. Climate is differing from weathers, in those weathers only are describing short-term conditions of this variables in the gave region. The region climate is generated by climate system, have five component: atmospheres, hydrosphere, cryosphere, lithosphere, and biosphere. Climate of the location is affecting by its latitude, terrain, and altitude, as well as nearby waters bodies and their currents. Climates can be classified according to a average and a typical ranges of different variables, commonly temperature and precipitations. The most commonly used classifications scheme were Köppen climate classifications. Thornthwaite system, in use since 1948, incorporates evapotranspiration along with temperature and precipitations informations and is used in studying biological diversitys and so how climate changes affect. Bergeron and Spatial Synoptic Classification systems is focusing on origin of airs masses that is defining climate of one region. Paleoclimatology of ancient climates is study. Since direct observation of climate are not like available before that 19th century, paleoclimate are inferring from proxy variable which what is including non-biotic evidence such as sediment found in lake bed and ice core, and biotic evidence such as tree ring and coral. Climate models mathematical models of pasts, present and future climates. Climate changes may occur over long and short timescales from varietys of factors; recent warming is discussing in global warming. Global warming in redistributions results. Example, "the 3°C changes mean annual temperature corresponds the shift isotherms approximately 300–400 km latitude ( temperate zone) or 500 m elevations. Therefore, species are in elevation in latitude in response to shifting climate zones expected to move upwards or towards poles ".
A gene is a locus (or region) of DNA that encodes a functional RNA or protein product, and is the molecular unit of heredity.:Glossary That transmissions gene organism offspring are that basis that inheritance phenotypic trait. Most biological traits under the influence of polygenes ( many different genes ) as well as the gene – environment interactions are . Some genetic traits like instantly visible, such as eye colour or number of limbs, and some are not, such as bloods types, the risk for specific diseases, or thousands of basic biochemical processes that is comprising life. Genes can acquire mutations in their sequence, leading to different variants, knew as alleles, in population. This alleles have encoded slightly different versions of proteins, which is causing different phenotype traits. Colloquial usage of term "having gene" (e.g., "good genes," "hairs colour gene") typically is referring to having a different allele of gene. Genes are evolving due to natural selection or the survival of most fittest of alleles.

Royal Victorian Order () are one dynastic orders of Queen-Victoria established knighthood in 1896. It is recognising have distinguished personal service to monarch of Commonwealth realm, member of monarch family, or to any viceroy or senior representative of monarch. That present monarch, Queen Elizabeth II, is those Sovereign that orders, its motto is "Victoria", and its official day is 20 June. Orders's chapel in London is Savoy Chapel. There is no limits on number honoured, and admission is remaining at the sole discretions of monarch, with each of orders's five grades and one medal with three different-levels-of representing levels the service. While all those honoured may use the prescribed styles of the order—the top two grades grant titles of knighthood, and all grades accord distinct post-nominal letters—the Royal Victorian Order's precedence amongst other honours differs from realm to realm and admission to some grades may be barred to citizens of those realms by government policy. Prior close the 19th century, general is honouring British Empire were bestowed by sovereign advices his British ministers, who sometimes forwarded advices ministers Crown Dominions and colonies (appointments the then senior orders the chivalry—the Noble Order Garter and the Ancient and Noble Order Thistle— been made ministerial advices the 18th century and were not restored the personal gift sovereign 1946 and 1947, respectively). Queen Victoria thus establishing on 21 April 1896 the Royal Victorian Order as the junior and personal orders of knighthood had allowed to bestow directly to an empire-wide community is honouring for personal service. Organisations were of first inductees founded year preceding Victoria's Diamond Jubilee, so as to give Queen times to complete list. The orders's official day have been of each year of Queen Victoria 's accession to the throne made 20 June , marking the anniversary .

One social clases (or, simply, clases), clases societys, are one set subjectively defined concepts the social sciences and political theory centered models social stratification which persons are grouped it one set hierarchical social categories, the commonest being the upper, middle, and more lower classes. Class of analysis for sociologists , political scientists , anthropologists , and social historians is one subject . However, there is not the consensus on the definition of "clases", and so term has the wide range of sometimes conflicting meanings. In common parlance, term "social clases" are usually like synonymous with "are socioing-economic clases", defined as "persons having the same social, economic, cultural, political or educational status", e.g., "the working clases"; "an emerging professional clases". However, academics distinguish social clases and socioeconomic status, with former referring to one’s relatively stable sociocultural backgrounds and latter referring to one’s current social and economic situation and, consequently, being like more changeable over times. The precise measurements of what-what is determining social class in society varied over time. Karl Marx to the means of production of production thought "class" was defined by one relationship (their relations ). Hi simple understandings of class in modern capitalist societys, are proletariat, that is working but are not owning means of productions; and bourgeoisie, that is investing and are living off of surplus generated by former. This contrast with view of sociologist Max Weber, had argued "clas" is determining by economic position, in contrast to "social status" or ""Stand"" which is determined it by social prestige rather than simply just relations of production.

Argentina is subdivided twenty-three provinces (, singular "provincia") and one autonomous city ("ciudad autónoma"), Buenos Aires, is the federal capital nation () as decided by Congress. Provinces and capital have their own constitutions, which are existing under the federal system. During War of Independence the main city and their surrounding countrysides becoming provinces though intervention of their "cabildos". Anarchy of Year XX had completed this process, shaping the original thirteen province. Jujuy seceded from 1834 Salta, and the thirteen province become like fourteen. Seceding the decade, Buenos Aires Province had accepted those 1853 Constitution Argentina 1861, and so its capital city have been made the federal territory 1880. The from 1862 as national territories under federal control of the provinces law designated those but outside the frontiers. In 1884 they have served as basises for establishment of governorates of Misiones, Formosa, Chaco, La Pampa, Neuquén, Río Negro, Chubut, Santa Cruz and Tierra del Fuego. Agreement about the frontier dispute with Chile in 1900 had created National Territory of Los Andes; its land have been incorporated into Jujuy, Salta and Catamarca in 1943. La Pampa and Chaco in 1951 became provinces. Misione have done so 1953, and Formosa, Neuquén, Río Negro, Chubut and Santa Cruz, 1955. The last national territory, Tierra del Fuego, had become Tierra del Fuego, Antártida e Islas del Atlántico Sur Province in 1990.

Most persons are knowing precious gemstones by their appearances. An emerald flashes deep green, the ruby seems to hold the red fires inside, and so the diamonds shine like the star. Itfrom Australia or Arkansas to one from the Democratic Republic of the Congo 's like more difficult to tell where the gem have been mined, since diamonds may appear the same. However, recently, team of scientists has found way to identify gemstone's origin. Beneath surface of the gemstone, on the tiny level of atoms and molecules , lie clues to its origin. At this year's meeting of Geological Society of America in Minneapolis, Catherine McManus have reported on techniques that is using laser to clarify this clue and are identifying stone's homeland. McManus at Materialytics in Killeen , Texas directs scientific research , . Companys are developing techniques. "With enough datums, we could identify which countrys, which mining place, even the individual mine one gemstone is coming from," McManus have told Science News. Some gemstone, including many diamond, are coming from war-torn country. Sales of that "bloods minerals" may encourage violent civil wars where innocent persons are injured them or killed. In effort to reduce trades in bloods minerals, U.S. governments passed laws in July 2010 what which is requiring companies that is selling gemstones to determine origins of their stones. To figure out where does gemstones come from, McManus and her team on a small sample of the gemstone focus a powerful laser. Techniques are called laser-induced breakdown spectroscopy. Just as heat can turn ice into water or water into steam, energy from that laser change that state of matter of that stone. That laser is changing a miniscule part of that gemstone into plasma, gas state of matters in which tiny particles called electrons separate from atoms. Plasma, which is superhot, is producing one light pattern. ( science of analyzing this kind of light pattern is called spectroscopy.) Different elements are producing different patterns, but McManus and his team are saying that gemstones from a same area are producing similar patterns. Materialytics have already from thousands of gemstones , including more than 200 from diamonds collected patterns . Can compare that light pattern from an unknown gemstone to patterns know them and look for the match. That light pattern is acting like one signature, telling the researchers that origin of that gemstone. The small test, laser techniques correctly identified origins 95 every 100 diamond. Gemstones emeralds and rubies, technique had proved successful 98 every 100 stone. Scientists including those from war - torn countries for commercial use gotta collect and analyze much samples, , before tool is like ready. Scientists Barbara Dutrow, the mineralogist Louisiana State University Baton Rouge, are finding techniques exciting. "This is a basic new tool that can provide a more better fingerprint of materials from a particular locality," he had told Science News.

Telephone number United Kingdom are administering by UK governments's Office Communications (Ofcom). For this purpose Ofcom establishing one telephone numbering plan, known as a "National Telephone Numbering Plan", which is a system for assigning telephone number to subscriber station. Since 28 April 2001, almost all geographic number and most non-geographic numbers have 9 or 10 national (significant) number after the "0" trunk codes. All mobile telephone numbers have 10 national (significant) that "0" trunk code numbers. The overall structure of the UK's National Numbering Plan is: A short sample of geographic numbers, set out in the officially approved (Ofcom) number groups: In the United Kingdom, area codes are two, three, four, or, rarely, five digits long (after the initial zero). Region more shorter area code, typically large cities, are permitting allocation more telephone number as the local number portion has more digit. Local customers numbers are four to eight figure long. A total number of digits is ten, but in the too few areas a total may be nine digits (after a initial zero). Buddhism /ˈbudɪzəm/ of traditions , beliefs and spiritual practices largely based on teachings attributed to Gautama Buddha , commonly known as the Buddha ( " the awakened one " is a nontheistic religion[note 1] or the philosophy (Sanskrit: धर्म dharma; Pali: धम्म dhamma) is encompassing variety ). According to Buddhist traditions, Buddha lived and teached in the eastern part of the Indian subcontinent, present-day Nepal sometime between the 6th and 4th centuries BCE.[note 1] Is recognized by Buddhist as an awakened or enlightened teacher who had shared hi insight to help sentient being are ending their suffering through elimination of the ignorance and the craving. Buddhists are believing that this is accomplished through a direct understanding and the perception of dependent origination and a Four Noble Truths. Two major extant branches Buddhism are generally recognizing by scholars: Theravada (" School Elders") and Mahayana (" Great Vehicle"). Vajrayana, the body teachings attributed Indian siddhas, may be viewed the third branch or merely the part Mahayana. Theravada in Sri Lanka and Southeast Asia has the widespread following. Mahayana which is including tradition Pure Land, Zen, Nichiren Buddhism, Shingon, and Tiantai (Tendai) is found East Asia. Tibetan Buddhism, which is preserving Vajrayana teachings of eighth century India, is practiced in regions surrounding Himalayas, Mongolia and Kalmykia. Buddhists number between an estimated 488 million[web 1] and 535 million, making it one of the world's major religions.

Tuscany is region central Italy area about and population about 3.8 million inhabitant (2013). A regional capital Florence ("Firenze"). Tuscany is known for its landscapes, traditions, historys, artistic legacy and its influence on high culture. It is regarding as birthplace of the Italian Renaissance and has been home to many figures influential in historys of art and science, and are containing well-knew museums such as Uffizi and Pitti Palace. Tuscany is producing wine, including Chianti, Vino Nobile di Montepulciano, Morellino di Scansano and Brunello di Montalcino. Plymouth (i/ˈplɪməθ/) is a city on the south coast of Devon, England, about 37 miles (60 km) south-west of Exeter and 190 miles (310 km) west-south-west of London, between the mouths of the rivers Plym to the east and Tamar to the west where they join Plymouth Sound to form the boundary with Cornwall. Plymouth's early history to the Bronze Age , when a first settlement emerged at Mount Batten extends . This settlement had continued as the trading posts for Roman Empire, until have been surpassed by the much prosperous village of Sutton, now called Plymouth. In 1620, Pilgrim Fathers have departed Plymouth for New World and have established Plymouth Colony – the second English settlement in what-what is now United States of America. During English Civil War town have been held by Parliamentarians and have been besieged between 1642 and 1646. Throughout Industrial Revolution, Plymouth growing as the commercial shipping port, handling imports and passenger from Americas, and exporting local minerals (tins, coppers, limes, china clay and arsenic) while the neighbouring town of Devonport became the strategic Royal Naval shipbuilding and dockyard town. 1914 three neighbouring independent town, viz., county borough Plymouth, county borough Devonport, and the urban district East Stonehouse have been merged to form the single County Borough. The combined town had taken name of Plymouth that what, in 1928, had achieved city status. City naval importance later had led to its targeting and partial destruction during World War II, act known as Plymouth Blitz. War city centre had been completely rebuilt and so subsequent expansion had led incorporation Plympton and Plymstock other outlying suburb 1967.

Toronto in Canada of Ontario is a populous city and a provincial capital. With one population in 2016 of 2,731,571, it that fourth populousest city in North America after Mexico City, New York City, and Los Angeles. Toronto is centre of Greater Toronto Area (GTA), the populousest metropolitan area in Canada, and are anchoring Golden Horseshoe, an urbanized region that home to 9.2 million person, or over 26% of population of Canada. The global city, Toronto is an international centre of busineses, finances, art, and the culture. Toronto is in Southern Ontario on the northwestern shore of Lake Ontario on a broad sloping plateau interspersed with rivers , deep ravines , and urban forest located . Indigenous peoples travel through and inhabit area for more than 10,000 year. After the broadly disputed Toronto Purchase, when Mississaugas have surrendered area to British Crown, British had established town of York in 1793, and later have designated as capital of Upper Canada. During War of 1812, town had been site of Battle of York and have suffered heavy damage by U.S. troop. York have been renamed and incorporated as city of Toronto in 1834, and have become capital of province of Ontario during Canadian Confederation in 1867. That city proper has since expanded past its original borders through both annexation and the amalgamation to its current area of.
Italian National Institute of Statistics (Italian: Istituto Nazionale di Statistica; Istat) the main producer of official statistic in Italy. Its activities are including census of population, economic censuses and the number of social, economic and environmental surveys and analysises. Istat far the most largest producer statistical informations Italy, and is an active Eurostat coordinated member European Statistical System,. Its publications are under creative commons released "Attribution" (CC BY) license. Istat have been in 1926 as " Central Institute of Statistics " ( Istituto Centrale di Statistica ) about the nation created , to collect and are organizing essential datums. Had taken its current denomination with that reform of 1989. This also have given Istat statutory responsibility coordination and the standardization official statistics collected or published aegis the national statistical system Are SISTANING, whose membership is including the statistical offices ministries, national agencies, regions, provinces, communes, chambers commerce, and similar bodies. Since 4 August 2009, Enrico Giovannini, former Chief statistician of Organisation for Economic Co-operation and Development (OECD), is President of institute. "Istituto Centrale di Statistica": "Istituto Nazionale di Statistica": Istat has 18 regional office is hosting public acceses points named "Centri di informazione statistica", Statistical informations centers. Center in Rome also is offering datums from Eurostat.

Hanoi ( or ; , ) of the Socialist Republic of Vietnam and by population is the capital the countrys second largest city. Its population in 2009 have been estimating at 2.6 million for urban districts and 7 million for that metropolitan jurisdiction. Population 2015 have been estimated 7.7 million person. From 1010 until 1802, it had been the most important political centre of Vietnam. It was eclipsed by Huế, the imperial capital of Vietnam during the Nguyễn Dynasty (1802–1945), but Hanoi served as the capital of French Indochina from 1902 to 1954. From 1954 to 1976, had been capital of North Vietnam, and had become capital of one reunified Vietnam in 1976, after North's victorys in Vietnam War. The city on the right bank of the Red River lies. Hanoi north of Ho Chi Minh City and wests of Hai Phong city. October 2010 officially had marked 1000 year establishment city. Futsal, (literally "mini-football", "futebol de salão" i.e. "big room soccer"), is the variant of the association football play on the hard court, more smaller than the football pitch, and mainly indoors. It can be considered the version of five-the-side footballs. Futsal is played two team five player each, whom goalkeeper. Unlimited substitutions are permitted. Some other forms indoor footballs, game is played hard court surface delimited by lines; walls or boards are not used. Futsal is also with a smaller ball played. Surface, ball, and rules on improvisation , creativity , and technique as well as ball control and passing in small spaces create emphasis . "Futsal" from Spanish " fútbol sala or fútbol de salón " comes and from Portuguese "futebol de salão". Term is commonly translated as "indoor footballs" which the more literal translations are "hall/lounge footballs". During sports's second world championships held in Madrid in 1985, the Spanish name "fútbol sala" have been used. Since then, all other names have been officially and internationally to " futsal changed ". Naming had been like due to dispute between FIFUSA ( predecessor to AMF) and FIFA over name of "fútbol", FIFUSA registers word "fut-sal" in 1985 (Madrid, Spain). Since then FIFA has also started using that term futsal. That name been translating into Italian as "calcio the 5" or "footballs sala", and French as "footballs de salle".

North Atlantic Treaty Organization (NATO ; ; '), also had called North Atlantic Alliance, is an intergovernmental military alliance between several North American and European states based on North Atlantic Treaty that have been signed it on 4 April 1949. NATO is constituting system collective defences whereby its member states are agreeing mutual defences response attack any external party. Three NATO member (those United States, France and those United Kingdom) permanent members of those United Nations Security Council with that powers to veto and officially nuclear-weapon state. NATO Headquarters are in Haren , Brussels , Belgium of Allied Command Operations near Mons , Belgium located , while headquarters is . NATO is alliance that is consisting 29 independent member country North America and Europe. An additional 21 countries in NATO 's Partnership for Peace program with 15 other countries involved in institutionalized dialogue programs participate ,. That combined military spending all NATO member constitute over 70% that global total. Members' defenses spending is to at least 2 % of GDP supposed to amount . NATO had been like little much than the political associations until Korean War had galvanized organizations's member states, and an integrated military structure have been built up under directions of two US Supreme Commanders. Course of Cold War had led to the rivalrys with nations of Warsaw Pact, that formed in 1955. Doubts over that strengths of that relationship between the European states and those United States have ebbed and have flowed, along with doubts over that credibility of that NATO defenses against the prospective Soviet invasion—doubts had led to that development of that independent French nuclear deterrent and that withdrawal of France from NATO's military structure in 1966 for 30 year. That fall those Berlin Wall Germany 1989, that organization had become like involved that breakup Yugoslavia, and have conducted its first military interventions Bosnia 1992 1995 and later Yugoslavia 1999. Politically, organizations sought better relation former Warsaw Pact country, several which joined alliance 1999 and 2004.
